            <div class="contents_style"><p><figure class="imageblock alignCenter" data-ke-mobileStyle="widthOrigin" data-filename="540pix-아이러브비즈니스 005 - 복사본.jpg" data-origin-width="540" data-origin-height="540"><a href="http://ilovebusiness.co.kr/" target="_blank"><img src="https://blog.kakaocdn.net/dna/cHB7ua/btryjGhiJkL/AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAI1vP7HDpX9_GyPvGiessxJPalIKdA_ed0sb14K1yMJi/img.jpg?credential=yqXZFxpELC7KVnFOS48ylbz2pIh7yKj8&expires=1790780399&allow_ip=&allow_referer=&signature=tho%2Bir1QN7ZOhuZh%2ByAJdzbBhNA%3D" srcset="https://img1.daumcdn.net/thumb/R1280x0/?scode=mtistory2&fname=https%3A%2F%2Fblog.kakaocdn.net%2Fdna%2FcHB7ua%2FbtryjGhiJkL%2FAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAI1vP7HDpX9_GyPvGiessxJPalIKdA_ed0sb14K1yMJi%2Fimg.jpg%3Fcredential%3DyqXZFxpELC7KVnFOS48ylbz2pIh7yKj8%26expires%3D1790780399%26allow_ip%3D%26allow_referer%3D%26signature%3Dtho%252Bir1QN7ZOhuZh%252ByAJdzbBhNA%253D" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='//t1.daumcdn.net/tistory_admin/static/images/no-image-v1.png'; this.srcset='//t1.daumcdn.net/tistory_admin/static/images/no-image-v1.png';" loading="lazy" width="540" height="540" data-filename="540pix-아이러브비즈니스 005 - 복사본.jpg" data-origin-width="540" data-origin-height="540"/></a></figure>
</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><b><u><span style="color: #008000;">외국인투자기업 목록<span> (</span>회사이름순<span>). : 2001-2500:</span></span></u></b></p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><b><u><span style="color: #008000;">no<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span><span style="color: #008000;">회사명<span>(</span>한글<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>회사명<span>(</span>영문<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>대표업종<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>대표투자국가<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>전화번호<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>주소<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>표준산업중분류명<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>표준산업분류명</span></u></b></p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2001<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스씨씨코리아<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>SCC KOREA LTD.<span> </span></span>종합스포츠시설기획운영업<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>일본<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>02-477-1268<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>서울특별시 강동구 강동대로<span> 245 </span>로얄빌딩<span> 208</span>호<span> (</span>성내동<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>여가<span>・</span><span>스포츠</span><span>・</span><span>오락</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>종합 스포츠시설 운영업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2002<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스씨요트<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span> <span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>보트<span>, </span>요트 수리 및 운영<span>, </span>기술자 파견<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>터키<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>033-610-7004<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>강원도 강릉시 강동면 헌화로<span> 950-39 (</span>신관<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>운수<span>・</span><span>창고</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>기타 수상 운송 지원 서비스업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2003<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스아이물류<span><span> </span>SI LOGISTICS CO.,LTD<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>물류사업<span>(</span>화물운송<span>, </span>통관 및 창고 보관업<span>)<span> </span></span>일본<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>051-832-3981<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>경남 진해시 안골동 부산항신항 북컨배후물류부지<span>4</span>단계이<span>4-1<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>운수<span>・</span><span>창고</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>일반 창고업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2004<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스아이씨코리아<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>SIC KOREA CO.,LTD.<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>전자제품 유통 도소매<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>중국<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>070-4801-6667<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>인천광역시 남동구 앵고개로<span>847</span>번길<span> 72 (</span>논현동<span>, </span>논현시티<span> 301</span>호<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>도<span>・</span><span>소매</span><span>(</span>유통<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>가전제품 및 부품 도매업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2005<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스아이트레이딩<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>SI Trading Co., Ltd.<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>중고 자동차 수출<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>예멘<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>인천광역시 연수구<span><span>&nbsp; </span></span>한나루로<span>158</span>번길<span> (</span>옥련동 송도쇼핑<span>) 5, 4</span>층<span> 402</span>호<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>도<span>・</span><span>소매</span><span>(</span>유통<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>중고 자동차 판매업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2006<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스알와이상사<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>SRY TRADING CO.,LTD<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>중고자동차 부품 수출<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>시리아<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>전라남도 나주시 노안면 율정길<span> ( </span>대명목재산업<span>) 150<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>도<span>・</span><span>소매</span><span>(</span>유통<span>)<span> </span></span>자동차 중고 부품 및 내장품 판매업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2007<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스알컴퍼니<span><span> </span>SR COMPANY CO., LTD.<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>레스토랑비지니스<span>, </span>부동산컨설팅<span>, </span>상품종합도매업<span><span> </span></span>태국<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>서울특별시 서초구<span><span>&nbsp; </span></span>남부순환로<span> (</span>양재동 성문빌딩<span>) 2636, 1</span>층<span> 101-2</span>호<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>숙박<span>・</span><span>음식점</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>기타 외국식 음식점업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2008<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스앤비<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>S &amp; B LTD<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>블라인드 제조업<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>중국<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>063-284-2437<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>전라북도 전주시 완산구 견훤왕궁<span>2</span>길<span> 26-9 (</span>중노송동<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>섬유<span>・</span><span>직물</span><span>・</span><span>의류</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>커튼 및 유사제품 제조업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2009<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스앤에프<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>S &amp; F CORPORATION<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>철구조물제조업<span>, </span>금속가공업<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>캐나다<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>061-461-1515<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>전라남도 영암군 삼호읍 대불산단<span>3</span>로<span> 171<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>금속<span>・</span><span>금속가공제품</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>그 외 기타 금속가공업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2010<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스앤와이코리아<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>SNY KOREA CORP.<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>섬유제조업<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>일본<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>7088459772<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>서울특별시 마포구 양화로<span> 175 </span>마젤란오피스텔<span> 612</span>호<span> (</span>동교동<span>)<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>섬유<span>・</span><span>직물</span><span>・</span><span>의</span>류<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>직물<span>, </span>편조원단 및 의복류 염색 가공업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2011<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스앤텍<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>S &amp; TECH CO.,LTD<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>기계<span>(</span>반도체금형<span>) </span>제조<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>캐나다<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>041-553-8411<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>충청남도 천안시 동남구 성남면 용원<span>3</span>길<span> 28<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>전기<span>・</span><span>전자</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>발광 다이오드 제조업</p>
<p data-ke-size="size16"><span>2012<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>(</span>주<span>)</span>에스에누씨<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>SNC CO.,LTD<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>금속조립 및 구조제 제조칠 판매업<span>,</span>도금업<span>,</span>무역업<span><span> </span></span>일본<span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span>055-327-0683<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>경상남도 김해시 유하로<span> 201 (</span>유하동<span>) -<span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>금속<span>・</span><span>금속가공제품</span><span><span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; </span></span>육상 금속 골조 구조재 제조업</p>

        var range = window.getSelection().getRangeAt(0);
